電機機械  
上銀(2049.TT) 
AI 與半導體需求強勁，精密線性傳動元件交期持續拉長

AI 與半導體需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環，上銀生產
的精密線性傳動元件大量用於 AI 與半導體設備 ， 在半導體產業CapEx
確立的情況下，預期這波拉貨潮將延續至明年。在上一波 2021-22 年
的上升循環中，股價最高來到當年度 EPS 的 45 倍，考量到目前僅出
現第一波漲價，研究部以 2027 年 EPS 13.16 元給予 35x 本益比，目
標價為 460 元，給予買進的評等。

1. 半導體設備需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環 
上銀生產的線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿大量應用於 AI 與半導體設備當中，這
波景氣上升循環由從 4Q25 開始，拉貨動能逐季提升。從交期來看，線
性滑軌的交期從去年年底 3 個月，拉長到現在的 4~5 個月；滾珠螺桿
交期從去年年底 4 個月，拉長到現在 5~6 個月。從營收來看，扣除 2
月份有過年因素外 (去年過年在 1 月)，上銀的月營收 YoY 從 3 月的
17.4%一路提升到 8 月的 42.3%，此期間沒有縮小過，顯示營收不但在
成長，成長的動能還在加速。公司亦提到從 6 月開始 BB raio 大於 1，
需求非常強勁，已開始規劃明年過年期間要如何加班生產。研究部看好
這波由 AI 與半導體帶動的工具機需求延續至明年，預估上銀 2026/27
年營收為 300.1/378.2 億元，+23.7%/+26.0% YoY。

2. 市場供不應求，交期持續拉長看好後續漲價機會 
市場供不應求 ， 公司於今年3 月宣布調漲 7~15%， 已於6 月開始反應 。
2Q26 毛利率 34.2%，季增 2.2 個百分點，為稼動率與價格同步提升所
致，預估 3Q26 漲價效應完全反應後毛利率將進一步提升至 35.2%，研
究部預估上銀 2026/27 年的毛利率為 34.3%/35.6%， 分別年增5.5/1.3
個百分點，EPS 為 9.56/13.16 元，+121.7%/+37.6% YoY。 
這波由半導體產業 CapEx 所帶動的景氣上升，研究部看好在交期不斷拉長
的情況下，後續還有漲價、上修毛利率的機會。(上一波 2021-2022 年上升
循環中毛利率最高來到 37-38%)

一、 一、公司介紹 
   上銀科技成立於 1989 年 ， 專注於線性滑軌 、 滾珠螺桿與單軸機器人等精密線性
傳動元件的製造 ， 為該領域的全球領導廠商之一 。 除了持續研發線性傳動產品以外 ，
也自行開發出多種半導體自動化設備 ，產品廣泛應用於汽車 、 半導體 、 醫療 、 國防 、
航太及食品等產業。上銀憑藉自有品牌 「HIWIN」 的深厚研發實力與品質行銷全世
界，目前為全球第二大生產線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿的廠商，第一大為日商 THK 
(6481.T)。1H26 產品營收占比﹔線性滑軌 60%、 滾珠螺桿20%、 工業機器人12%、
其他 8%。2025 年地區營收占比﹔台灣 44%、中國 21%、德國 16%、美國 5%、
日本 4%、其他 10%。(地區營收 2025 年已是最新公布)

二、 產業分析 
上銀主要產品為線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿，產品功能如下﹔ 
1. 線性滑軌﹔一種線性傳動元件，由滑塊與滑軌組成，透過內部的滾珠或滾  
柱循環系統來降低摩擦，讓平台保持平行穩定的直線運動。  
2. 滾珠螺桿﹔一種線性傳動元件，由螺桿、螺帽與鋼珠組成，利用鋼珠在螺桿與
螺帽間循環滾動，將旋轉運動轉換為直線運動。

圖四、滾珠螺桿

資料來源：上銀官網

商業模式 
線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿為工具機零組件 ， 工具機產業商業模式分為以下三種 (以 AOI
檢測設備為例)﹔ 
1. 工具機零組件廠生產線性滑軌 ➨ 工具機 OEM 廠將各零組件組裝成五軸加工
機 ➨ 設備零組件廠用五軸加工機生產高精度定位平台  ➨ 設備商用高精度定位
平台與其他零組件組裝成 AOI 檢測設備。  
2. 工具機零組件廠生產線性滑軌 ➨ 工具機 OEM 廠將各零組件組裝成五軸加工
機 ➨ 設備商用五軸加工機自行生產所需的高精度定位平台或其他零組件。 
3. 工具機零組件廠生產的線性滑軌同時是工具機零組件與設備零組件  ➨ 設備
商直接購買線性滑軌來組裝。

圖五、商業模式

資料來源：統一投顧整理

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 5

產業趨勢 
JMTBA (日本工具機協會) 每月會將日本所有工具機 OEM 廠的訂單加總 ， 公布 「日
本工具機訂單金額」 ，其產品包含多軸加工機、CNC 車床、滾齒機……等等，由於
日本擁有全球規模前幾大的工具機 OEM 廠，如 DMG、Yamazaki、Okuma，且多
年來產品遍布各個產業所累積的經驗也使得這些公司不論在自研控制器的演算
法，還是機器長時間運轉下維持的精度與溫度都與其他國家有技術上的差距。  
因此，在各行各業景氣轉好，需要大量的工具機時，JMTBA 公布的訂單金額被視
為是工具機產業最重要的觀察指標，工具機 OEM 廠訂單增加 ➨ 增加向工具機
零組件廠的拉貨。 
觀察過去十年日本工具機訂單的走勢，可發現景氣大約呈現兩年上升、兩年下降
的循環。上一次的上升週期為 2021-22 年疫情加上塞港，全球各產業瘋狂拉貨，
使得工具機訂單 YoY 從 2020/11 的-6%，最高拉升到 2021/5 的 142%，一直到
2022/10 訂單 YoY 才轉負 。 塞港造成的恐慌式補庫存為工具機產業帶來20 年來不
曾有過的成長率 ， 也使得隨後各產業去化庫存時 ， 工具機這次的景氣休息得較久 ，
直到 2H25 才再次迎來上升週期。

圖六、日本工具機訂單及 YoY

資料來源：JMTBA

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 6

圖七、上銀過去十年股價走勢圖

資料來源：Wind 
（註：與日本工具機訂單 YoY 高度聯動）

而在此波的上升週期，生產線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿等工具機零組件全球市占前二的
廠商，THK、上銀一致提到這一次的需求來自 AI 與半導體，且需求非常強勁。 
細看 AI 與半導體產業，其中涵蓋了相當多工具機的蹤跡，舉凡伺服器散熱所需的
水冷板、快接頭及熱交換器，到伺服器機殼、Compute/GPU Tray 與各種結構件，
都需要以工具機進行切削或沖壓。半導體產業中，在製程升級與產能擴充的過程
需要大量的半導體設備 ，不論在前 、 中 、 後段的設備都須使用線性傳動元件 ， 如蝕
刻機、AOI 檢測設備、各類型 Bonder 等等。 
產品規格方面，AI 與半導體所需的工具機精度更高，使得 THK、上銀這種技術領
先的廠商進一步受惠 。 一般工業用工具機僅需使用H/N 級的線性滑軌與 C5/C7 級
的滾珠螺桿，生產水冷板與快接頭時需使用 SP/P 級的線性滑軌與 C3 級的滾珠螺
桿；在Die Bonder (晶粒鍵合機)與 Flip Chip Bonder (倒裝鍵合機)上則需使用 UP/SP
級的線性滑軌與 C0/C1 級的滾珠螺桿，生產高精度的線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿毛利率
顯著高於精度較低的。

表二、線性滑軌規格

SP ≈ 7 μm 
P ≈ 15 μm 
H ≈ 30 μm 
N ≈ 50 μm

除了做元件以外，上銀也與大銀微系統合作製造半導體整機設備，由上銀做線性
滑軌與滾珠螺桿 ，大銀微提供各類馬達 、 定位平台 、 驅動器與運動控制器等等 ，產
品包含 EFEM ( 晶圓移載系統 )、Load Port ( 晶圓裝卸機)、Wafer Robot ( 晶圓機器
人) 、Wafer Aligner (晶圓尋邊器)，目前認列於機器人營收當中，產能滿載，直接
出貨給半導體製程廠商。

小結 
歷經 2022 年後的全球各產業庫存去化，工具機景氣委靡了接近三年的期間，如
今挾帶著 AI 伺服器與半導體設備需求強勁成長，再次迎來新一波景氣上升循環。
從水冷板、快接頭到各類半導體設備，皆帶動工具機需求快速升溫， 相關廠商也
同步指出需求強勁、訂單持續增加，交期明顯拉長。 
更重要的是，這波需求主要來自半導體設備等高精度應用領域，所需線性滑軌與
滾珠螺桿規格高於一般工業產品，上銀除受惠於工具機景氣重新向上外，亦可進
一步受惠於高規格精密線性傳動元件需求成長。

### Page 9

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 9

三、 三、營運狀況 
1. 1. 半導體設備需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環 
上銀生產的線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿大量應用於半導體設備當中，這波景氣上升循
環由從 4Q25 開始，拉貨動能逐季提升。從交期來看，線性滑軌的交期從去年
年底 3 個月 ，到五月法說會提到 3.5 個月 ， 再到八月份交期已經拉長到 5 個月 ；
滾珠螺桿交期從去年年底 4 個月，到五月法說會的 4.5 個月，再到現在的 6 個
月。從營收來看，扣除 2 月份有過年因素外 (去年過年在 1 月)，上銀的月營收
YoY 從 3 月的 17.4%一路提升到 8 月的 42.3%，此期間沒有縮小過，顯示營收
不但在成長，成長的動能還在加速。公司亦提到從6 月開始 BB raio 大於 1，需
求非常強勁，已開始規劃明 年過年期間要如何加班生產。研究部看好這波半導
體帶動的工具機需求延續至明年 ，預估上銀 2026/27 年營收為 300.1/378.2 億
元，+23.7%/+26.0% YoY。

圖十二、上銀各產品交期

資料來源：統一投顧整理

2. 2. 市場供不應求，交期持續拉長看好後續漲價機會 
市場供不應求，公司於今年 3 月宣布調漲 7~15%，已於 6 月開始反應。2Q26 毛
利率 34.2%，季增 2.2 個百分點，為稼動率與價格同步提升所致，預估 3Q26 漲價
效應完全反應後毛利率將進一步提升至 35.2%，研究部預估上銀 2026/27 年的毛
利率為 34.3%/35.6%，分別年增 5.5/1.3 個百分點 ，EPS 為 9.56/13.16 元，
+121.7%/+37.6% YoY。這波由半導體產業 CapEx 所帶動的景氣上升，研究部看
好在交期不斷拉長的情況下，後續還有漲價、上修毛利率的機會。 (上一波 2021-
2022 年上升循環中毛利率由 31%迅速拉升至 37-38%)

3. 預期景氣將延續至明年，目前評價偏低 
AI 與半導體需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環，上銀生產的精密線性傳動
元件大量用於 AI 與半導體設備，在半導體產業 CapEx 確立的情況下，預期這波
拉貨潮將延續至明年。在上一波 2021-22 年的上升循環中，股價最高來到當年度
EPS 的 45 倍，考量到目前僅出現第一波漲價，研究部以 2027 年 EPS 13.16 元給
予 35x 本益比，目標價為 460 元，給予買進的評等。

附錄 一 ：評等之標準  
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25 %以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～ -10%

提 供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見，不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其

## Document 060: 2330台積電 群益

Source PDF: all report pdf/2330台積電 群益.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-09
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

1 群益投顧 
ASML (ASML US) 與台積電 (2330 TT) 共同推動 High NA EUV 將自現行的 6 吋光罩，
轉移至更大的 12 吋 光 罩。 目標於 2031 年之前建立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ， 並 於
2033 年之前實現微影系統全面就緒。台積電並表示自 2030 年 起 ， 將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產
中導入 ASML 的 High NA  EUV 。台積電與 ASML 共同發起 6 吋光罩轉移至 12 吋光
罩 的 規 格 轉 移 ， 其 戰 略 意 義 在 於 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積 電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。 因 此
對 台 積 電 的 投 資 評 等 Buy ，目標價 3,200 元。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

ASML 與 台 積 電 共 同 推 動 High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋 光 罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大
的 12 吋 光 罩 ：  
09/07/2026 ASML 與 台 積 電 於 BACUS 國 際 光 學 工 程 學 會(SPIE) 先 進 微 影 技
術 研 討 會 前 宣 布 發 起 產 業 合 作 ， 共 同 推 動High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋光
罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大 的 12 吋 光 罩 ， 預 期 將 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 的 生 產 力 、 降 低 晶
片 製 造 成 本 ， 並 消 除 需 要 2 張 6 吋 光 罩 在 High NA EUV 曝 光 時 進 行 拼 接 的
限 制，使 整 個 半 導 體 產 業 受 惠。此 轉 移 能 讓 先 進 晶 片 製 造 服 務 提 供 者 充 分 利
用 High NA EUV 的 優 勢 ， 更 提 升 未 來 世 代 先 進 晶 片 的 成 本 效 益 。 
目標於 2031 年 之 前 建 立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ，並於 2033 年 之 前 實
現 微 影 系 統 全 面 就 緒 ， 以 支 援 先 進 製 程 量 產 。 台 積 電 並 表 示 自2030 年 起 ，
將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產 中 導 入 ASML 的 High NA 技 術 。  
12 吋光罩 使 High NA 效 率 提 升 ：  
現行 EUV 設備的 NA(Numerical Aperture ， NA) 為 0.33 ， High NA 則 提 高 到
0.55 。 NA 數 值 越 高 ， 解 析 度 也 越 高 、 曝 光 時 能 呈 現 的 細 節 愈 多 。ASML 資
料顯示 High NA 可 將 最 小 解 析 尺 寸 從 現 行 EUV 的約 13 奈 米 進 一 步 縮 小 到
8 奈 米 ， 也 就 是 能 在 晶 圓 上 印 出 更 細 微 的 電 路 圖 形 。 部 分 原 本 需 要2 次 甚 至
更多次曝光才能完成的圖形，也有機會改以單次曝光處理，降低製程複雜
度 、 缺 陷 風 險 與 生 產 周 期 。 
舉 例 來 說 ，0.33 NA EUV 機 台 ， 至 少 需 要 4 張 光 罩 、 分 多 次 曝 光 才 能 完 成
A14 、 A10 金 屬 層 圖 形 ；High NA EUV ， 只 要 1 張 光 罩 就 能 完 成 相 同 圖 形 。
但 解 析 度 提 高 ，High NA 的 光 學 設 計 讓 單 次 可 曝 光 範 圍 縮 小 到 原 本EUV 的
50% ， 製 作 大 顆 晶 片 時 可 能 必 須 分 成2 次 曝 光 ， 再 把 圖 形 拼 接 起 來 。 這 也 是
台積電與 ASML 此 次 推 動 12 吋 光 罩 的 原 因 ， 相 較 現 行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 放 大 光 罩
尺 寸 預 期 能 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 生 產 力、降 低 晶 片 製 造 成 本，並 消 除 High NA
曝 光 時 的 拼 接 限 制 。 
台 積 電 預 計 2030 年 開 始 使 用 High NA  EUV ， 推估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或
A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採 用：  
台積電預計 2030 年 開 始 導入 High NA  EUV ，比 12 吋 光 罩 微 影 系 統 預 計 全
面就緒的 2033 年 更 早 。 這 代 表 台 積 電 初 期 導 入High NA 時 ， 仍 會 先 使 用 現
行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 之 後 再 隨 12 吋 光 罩 與 相 關 系 統 成 熟 逐 步 轉 換 。

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

2 群益投顧 
根 據 台 積 電 目 前 公 開 的 製 程 藍 圖 ，A13 與 A12 製 程 都 預 計 2029 年 量 產 。 其
中 A13 是 A14 平 台 的 延 伸 ，A12 則 將 超 級 電 軌(Super Power Rail) 背面供電
技術導入 A14 平 台 。 因 此 N2 、 A16 、 A14 、 A13 與 A12 都 早 於 台 積 電 預 計
2030 年 導 入 High NA ， 推 估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或 A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採
用。  
Intel (INTC US) 和 Samsung (005930 KS) 領 先 台 積 電 採 用 High NA EUV ，但
也 同 時 參 與 12 吋 光 罩 開 發 ：  
Intel 在 2024 年 宣 布 完 成 全 球 首 套 商 用 High NA EUV 設 備 的 組 裝 ；2026 年
High NA 已 經 正 式 進 入 量 產 ， 並 用 於 部 分 18A 製 程 層 。 是 目 前 High NA 導
入 進 度 最 快 的 晶 片 製 造 商 。Samsung 則預計 2028 年導入 DRAM 量 產 ； 台 積
電選擇在 2030 年 導 入 。Intel 和 Samsung 也 同 時 表 示 與 ASML 及 供 應 鏈 合
作 ， 推 動 未 來 轉 向 大 尺 寸 光 罩 。 
台 積 電 與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，將 領 導 業
界 規 格 ：  
6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋光罩 的 這 項 升 級 非 單 純 的 尺 寸 放 大 ， 而 是 面 臨 生 態 系
與 物 理 極 限 的 多 重 挑 戰 。 整 體 供 應 鏈 重 構 ， 包 括 基 板 與 材 料 以 及 設 備 等 等 。
12 吋 光 罩 石 英 基 板 的 平 坦 度 、 缺 陷 密 度 與 熱 膨 脹 係 數 控 制 難 度 倍 增 。 製 程
設 備 如 薄 膜 沉 積、蝕 刻、清 洗 與 自 動 化 搬 運 設 備 皆 需 重 新 開 發。後 續 檢 測 與
量測極限檢測難度也增加，更大面積的圖形缺陷檢測（ Inspection ）與修補
技 術 需 要 突 破 。 在 12 吋 面 積 上 維 持 奈 米 級 的 對 位 精 度 與 線 寬 均 勻 性 極 為 困
難   
電 子 設 計 自 動 化 (EDA) 工 具 必 須 重 新 適 應 大 尺 寸 曝 光 視 場 與 資 料 處 理 。 12 
吋 光 罩 的 圖 形 檔 案 與 運 算 複 雜 度 將 大 幅 上 升 。 
台積電與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，其 戰略意
義在於 ，台 積 電 為 全 球 最 大 晶 圓 代 工 廠，為 先 進 製 程 最 大 的 供 應 商，可 以 領
導 光 罩 基 底 供 應 商 、 光 罩 檢 測 設 備 商， 甚 至 EDA 廠 等 整 個 生 態 系 ，除 共同
分攤 ， 跨 越 升 級 12 吋 光 罩 的 龐 大 研 發 成 本外 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積
電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。因 此 對 台 積 電 的投 資 評 等 Buy ， 目 標 價 3,200 元。

相 關 個 股 獲 利 預 估 、 評 價 及 投 資 建 議

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

2357.TT 華碩 
 2H26 AI 動能續強

[財務資訊更新]

公司簡介 
華碩電腦成立於 1990 年，以生產個人電腦主機板起家，後來跨入筆記型電腦、無線
網通、及 3C 消費電子產品領域，以自有品牌「ASUS」銷售全球。2006 年以 ROG 
(Republic of Gamers) 品牌發展高階電競主機板及筆電等產品，樹立在電競市場的地
位，近年亦開始發展 AI 伺服器、AIoT 產品，搶攻 AI 商機。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 1,130 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，840 
【大盤指數/股價】47183 / 979

➢ 2H26 AI 伺服器強勢成長  
華碩 8 月營收 949.6 億元 ，MoM+12.9%，YoY+51.2%， 公司預期3Q26 
PC 營收 QoQ+15%~20% ， YoY+20%~30% ， 零 組 件 營 收  
QoQ+5%~10%、YoY 持平，伺服器營收 QoQ+10%，YoY+150%。雖
然 PC 產業面臨缺料與關鍵零組件成本上升的挑戰，但記憶體的漲價帶
動 PC 售價上升，加上華碩高價值產品(如:高階電競、Copilot+PC 等)已
占營收 65%以上，此類產品的消費者價格敏感度較低，因此公司認為
3Q26 PC 業務價、量都優於 2Q26。 
華碩 AI 伺服器業務持續強勁 ， 客戶包含CSP、Neo cloud 以及主權 AI，
公司的伺服器營收當中，90%由 AI 貢獻(AI 中 HGX 約占營收 65%、
GB300 約占營收 35%)，預期伺服器 2026 全年營收 YoY+150%。投研
部估計華碩 3Q26 營收 2,773.6 億元，QoQ+7.6%，YoY+38.5%，毛利
率 16.3%，EPS 25.10 元。  
➢ IFA 2026 展出搭載 NVIDIA RTX Spark 的 ProArt PC 
華碩於 IFA 2026 展前發表會中 ， 已發表搭載 RTX Spark 的 ProArt P16、
ProArt P14、ProArt GR1X mini PC：1) ProArt P16 機身厚度 12.9 mm、
重量 1.77kg， 相較前一代薄14%、輕 9%， 為該系列歷來為最輕薄的16
吋版本；2) ProArt P14 機身厚度 13.9 mm、重量 1.48kg，搭載 90Wh
電池提供全天候續航；3) ProArt GR1X 尺寸 150×150×51 mm ，主打
機身僅掌心大小 ，同時兼具強大AI 運算效能 。 華碩表示，ProArt P16 與
ProArt P14 首波已預購一空，新品將於 4Q26 出貨。關於 AI PC 市場，
華碩之 AI PC 2Q26 已在消費型 NB 營收中達到 30%占比，公司認為未
來 2~3 年 AI PC、Edge AI 將引發產品升級循環 ， 對2H26 亦樂觀看待 。 
➢ 建議買進，目標價 1,130 元 
華碩 2H26 在 AI 伺服器與 AI PC 的帶動下展望樂觀，預期 2027 年 AI
伺服器能為公司帶來穩定的成長動能 。 華碩歷史PE 區間為 9X~18X，考
量公司營運展望正向 ， 投研部認為有利帶動其PE 回升 ， 買進目標價1,130
元(2027F PE 13X)。

2H26 AI 伺服器強勢成長 
華碩 8 月營收 949.6 億元，MoM+12.9%，YoY+51.2%，公司預期 3Q26 PC 營收

新台幣 483 億元)的海外無擔保可轉換公司債(ECB)，並且計畫以研華(2395)持股發行

輕薄 NB 與高效能迷你桌上型電腦，目標客戶為創作者、AI 開發者、電競玩家。

條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保證。非經本公司同意，不得

2382 廣達電腦(Quanta)

報 告 日 期 ：2026/09/08           研 究 員 ：曾 智 楷(Kevin  Tseng ) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一、 2Q26 營收 、獲利創高， EPS 7.43 元 
廣達 2Q26 營收 1.04 兆， QoQ +28.1% ，YoY +105.6% ，受 惠於
伺 服 器、 筆電 第二季 均 呈 現雙 位 數 的成長 。 產 品 組 合 部 分 ， 伺 服
器 佔 整 體 營 收 8 成 ， 其 中 ，AI 伺 服 器 對 整 體 伺 服 器 營 收 貢 獻約
75%~80% ，筆電 則佔 整體營收的 低雙位數百分比。 筆電部分，
2Q26 筆 電 出 貨 量1,150 萬 台 ，QoQ + 15% ，YoY  -5%。2Q26 筆
電 出 貨 優 於預 期 ， 主 要 由 於客 戶 新 機 種 的 拉 貨 動 能 延 續 至 第 二
季， 以及 各 品 牌 客 戶預期 記 憶 體 價 格 持 續 上 漲而 提 前 拉 貨。 而獲
利 方 面，2Q26 營 業 毛 利520 .8 億，QoQ +34.6 %。YoY + 46.6 %，
毛 利 率 5.0%，QoQ +0.2ppts，YoY -2.0ppts，營 業 利 益342 .4 億，
QoQ +48.7 %，YoY +67.8 %，營 利 率 3.3 %，QoQ +0.45 ppts，YoY 
-0.75 ppts 。公 司 表 示，高階 AI 機櫃對 公司 毛 利 的 稀 釋，隨著 產品
組合優化 影響 逐步 降 低 ， 以 及 2Q26 費 用 率 降 至 歷 史 新 低 的
1.72% ， 帶 動 營 利 率 獲 得 改 善 。2Q26 業 外 淨 利 23.2 億 ， 稅 後 淨
利 286.5 億， QoQ +35.2 %，YoY + 69.9 %， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.43
元。

二、 3Q26  AI 伺服器動能延續，筆電面臨 逆風  
公司 公告 7 月 營 收 3399 億， MoM -6.3% ，YoY +120.7% ，今年
累 計 營 收2.21 兆， YoY +79.6% 。展望 3Q26 ， 公司 預期 AI 伺服
器 營 收 持 續 強 勁 增 長 ， 但 因 第 二 季 基 期 已 拉 高 ， 季 增 幅 度 會 低 於
第 二 季 。此 外 ， 公 司 表 示 ，第 三 季 起部 分 客 戶 專 案將由 Buy and Sell 
模 式 轉 為Consignment 模式，將 有 助 於 大 幅 減 輕 營 運 資 金 壓 力 並
舒 緩 毛 利 率 稀 釋影響 。 筆 電 部 分 ， 由 於 主 要客 戶 已 於 上 半 年 提 前
拉 貨，再加上 記 憶 體 與CPU 成 本 大 漲，壓 抑 終 端 採 購 意 願，預計
第 三 季 出 貨 量 將 季 減 逾20% 。 預估 3Q26 營收 1.05 兆 ，QoQ 
+1.5 %，YoY + 112.5 %， 毛 利 率 5.1%， 營 利 率 3.3%， 稅 後 淨 利
288 億 元 ， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.46 元。

三、預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 27.50 /31.63 元 
展望 2026 年 ， 公 司 表 示 ，AI 伺 服 器 訂 單 能 見 度 已到 2028 年，
維持 今年 全年 AI 伺 服 器營 收 倍 增預期 不 變，而 通 用 伺 服 器亦 維持
全 年 營 收 雙 位 數 年 增 目 標 。筆 電 部 分 ，儘 管 上 半 年 連 續 兩 季 出 貨
表 現 優 於 預 期 ，但 由 於客 戶 提 前 備 貨且 缺 料 情 況在 下 半 年 未 見 明
顯改善 ，公司 預 估 下 半 年 出 貨 將 低 於 上 半 年 ，全 年 筆 電 出 貨 量 預
投資評等：買進

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
廣 達 電 腦(Quanta) 為 全 球 筆 記 型 電 腦 專 業 研 發 設 計 製 造 與 雲 端 運
算 解 決 方 案 領 導 供 應 商 ，為 全 球 前 五 大 筆 記 型 電 腦 代 工 廠 商 。 公
司 主 要 替 品 牌 業 者 從 事 代 工 服 務 ， 代 工 項 目 包 含 筆 記 型 電 腦 、 平
板 電 腦 、 智 慧 型 手 機 、 伺 服 器 、 商 用 及 家 用 智 慧 物 聯 網 裝 置 、 消
費 性 電 子 產 品 、 車 用 電 子 等 。 公 司 總 部 位 於 桃 園 龜 山 ， 生 產 基 地
包 含 桃 園 、 大 陸 上 海 、 重 慶 、 江 蘇 、 美 國 田 納 西 州 、 加 州 、 墨 西
哥 、 德 國 、 泰 國 等 。 公 司2025 年 伺 服 器 業 務(包含 AI 伺服器 )佔
整 體 營 收 比 重 近80% ， 筆 電(PC) 及 其 他 業 務 佔 約20% 。202 5 年
銷 售 地 區 比 重 ： 美 國73%、 大 陸 5%、 荷 蘭 2%、 日 本 3%、 其 他
17%。 筆 記 型 電 腦 主 要 客 戶 為Apple 、HP、Dell 、 華 碩 、 宏 碁 ，
伺 服 器 客 戶 包 含Microsoft 、Google 、Amazon 、Meta ， 而 車 用 客

及 英 業 達 等 。

二. 營運概況及展望  
圖 一 、 廣 達 製 造 基 地 及 全 球 據 點

資 料 來 源 ：廣 達 、 宏 遠 投 顧 彙 整

1. 四大 CSP 上修 今年 資本支出達 7,450 億美元  
在 生 成 式AI 商 機 快 速 擴 張 帶 動 下，全 球 雲 端 基 礎 設 施 投 資 進 入 新
一 輪 高 峰 。 亞 馬 遜 、Alphabet 和 微 軟 認 為 其 在 先 前 的 鉅 額 投 資 已
帶 動 營 收 明 顯 成 長 ， 同 時 預 估 未 來AI 潛 在 商 機 仍 持 續 擴 大 。 其 中，
亞 馬 遜、Alphabet 和 Meta 已 陸 續 上 調2026 全 年 投 資 金 額，且 四
大雲端服務供應商 (CSP )總資本支出將上升至 7,450 億美元新高
規模 。 亞 馬 遜 、 微 軟 、Alphabet 與 Meta 2026 年第 1、2 季分別
為 1,306 億、1,701 億 美 元，同 時 亞 馬 遜、Alphabet 和 Meta 再上
修 2026 全 年 資 本 支 出 金 額，2026 年 資 本 支 出預 估 將達 7,450 億
美元 。而 根據 TrendForce 最 新 產 業 研 究，全 球 主 要 雲 端 服 務 供 應
商(CSP) 為 加 速 建 置AI 基 礎 設 施 ， 資 本 支 出 預 估 於2026 年 將 年
增 98%，2027 年 再 成 長50% 至 1.3 兆 美 元 左 右，年 增 率 收 斂的 主
要 原因， 除了 反 映 高 基 期 因 素之外 ， 部 分 可 歸 咎 為鉅 額 資 本 支 出
對 四 大 CSP 財 務 衝 擊 日 益 擴 大。然 而， 在 AI Inference 、Agentic 
AI 、 自 研 ASIC 和 新 一 代AI 模 型 持 續 推 升 算 力 需 求 的 情 況 下 ， 預
期 整 體 CSP 投 資 金 額 將 續 創 新 高 紀 錄 ， 產 業 成 長 有 望 從 原 先 以
GPU 擴 張 為 主，轉 為 包 含AI server 、液 冷 散 熱、先 進 封 裝、高 速
互 聯 、 電 源 和 記 憶 體 等 基 礎 設 施 全 面 升 級 的 態 勢 。

圖 二 、2024 -2027 年 全 球 九 大CSP 資 本 支 出 總 額 預 估(單位 :十 億 美 元)

資 料 來 源 ：TrendForce 、 宏 遠 投 顧 整 理

2. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31%  
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產 業 研 究 ， 近 期 超 大 型CSP 、
Tier -2 資 料 中 心 業 者 對NVIDIA GB/VR 等 整 櫃 式AI server 方 案 的
採 購 意 願 明 顯 提 高 ， 且Google 、AWS 新 一 代 ASIC 平 台 於 2026
下 半 年 陸 續 放 量，加 上 中 系 業 者 擴 大 採 用 本 地AI 方 案 以 滿 足 雲 端
大 型 語 言 模 型(LLM ) AI 需 求 ， 促 使TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出 貨 年 成 長 幅 度，從 原 先 的28% 更 新 為 近31% 。北 美 五 大
業者 2026 年 的 資 本 支 出 多 翻 倍 提 升 ， 反 映 出 大 型CSP 廠 持 續 擴
建 AI 資 料 中 心、GPU 叢 集、液 冷 散 熱 等 新 一 代 基 礎 設 施，以 應 對
生成式 AI 及 大 型 模 型 快 速 成 長 帶 來 的 算 力 需 求 。其中， Google 
2026 至 2027 年 都 將 側 重 自 家TPU 晶 片 發 展 ，2027 年 出 貨 量 有
望 繼 續 高 速 成 長 。AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為 GPU AI 
server 主 力，自 研 ASIC 也 穩 定 出 貨，預 期 至2027 年 量 能 將 再 擴
大。 Meta 2026 年 主 要 採 用NVIDIA GB/VR 、AMD Helios 整櫃式
方 案，自 研 ASIC 布 局 預 計 至2027 年 將 明 顯 加 速，為 該 公 司 長 期
降低 AI 基 礎 設 施 成 本 、 提 升 推 論 效 率 的 重 要 策 略 。

3. 2Q26 PC 出貨量 年減 4.9% ，蘋果 市佔率逆勢擴張  
根據 國 際 數 據 資 訊(IDC )報 告 ，2026 年 第 二 季 全 球PC 出 貨 總 量 達
6,820 萬 台、年 減 4.9% ，主 要 由 於 記 憶 體 短 缺 持 續 推 升 零 組 件 成
本 ， 再 加 上 總 體 經 濟 環 境 不 佳 ， 導 致 終 端 需 求 與 換 機 意 願 大 幅 放
緩，整 體 拉 貨 動 能 無 力 延 續。其 中，Apple 2Q26 PC 出 貨 量 為670
萬 台 ，YoY +10.1% ， 主 要 受 惠 於MacBook Neo 帶 動 。 此 外 ， 面
對 零 組 件 短 缺 與 成 本 攀 升 逆 風 ， 蘋 果 憑 藉 龐 大 採 購 規 模 與 供 應 鏈
優 勢 穩 固 供 貨 ， 使 其 市 佔 率 逆 勢 擴 張 。

一、3Q26  AI 伺服器動能延續，筆電面臨 逆風  
公 司 公 告7 月 營 收 3399 億， MoM -6.3% ，YoY +120.7% ，今年
累 計 營 收2.21 兆， YoY +79.6% 。 展 望 3Q26 ， 公 司 預 期AI 伺服
器 營 收 持 續 強 勁 增 長 ， 但 因 第 二 季 基 期 已 拉 高 ， 季 增 幅 度 會 低 於
第 二 季 。此 外 ， 公 司 表 示 ，第 三 季 起 部 分 客 戶 專 案將由 Buy and Sell 
模 式 轉 為Consignment 模式，將 有 助 於 大 幅 減 輕 營 運 資 金 壓 力 並
舒 緩 毛 利 率 稀 釋影 響 。 筆 電 部 分 ， 由 於 主 要客 戶 已 於 上 半 年 提 前
拉 貨，再加上 記 憶 體 與CPU 成 本 大 漲，壓 抑 終 端 採 購 意 願，預 計
第 三 季 出 貨 量 將 季 減 逾20% 。 預估 3Q26 營收 1.05 兆， QoQ 
+1.5% ，YoY +112.5% ， 毛 利 率 5.1% ， 營 利 率 3.3% ， 稅 後 淨 利
288 億 元 ， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.46 元。

全 年 營 收 雙 位 數 年 增 目 標 。筆 電 部 分 ，儘 管 上 半 年 連 續 兩 季 出 貨
表 現 優 於 預 期 ，但由 於 客 戶 提 前 備 貨且 缺 料 情 況在 下 半 年 未 見 明
顯改善 ，公司 預 估 下 半 年 出 貨 將 低 於 上 半 年 ， 全 年 筆 電 出 貨 量 預
估 將 較 去 年 呈 現 雙 位 數 下 滑 。而 營 利 率 部 分 ， 公 司 表 示 ， 由 於下
半年 AI 佔 比 持 續 提 升，有 助 於 產 品 組 合 優 化，預 估 全 年 利 潤 率 可
維 持 在 與 上 半 年 相 當 的 水 準 。另 外 ， 在 產 能 佈 局 方 面 ， 公 司上調

Airtac
Valuation
We derive our target price of NT$1,750 for Airtac by using a P/E methodology, as we believe the margin and earnings growth
outlooks will be the key drivers of the stock. We apply a P/E of 27.5x for 2H26-1H27E, which we derive from the mid range of
prior upcycle valuation 25-35x during 2021 in view of a strong upcycle with solid FA demand and ongoing share gains.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the Airtac stock from reaching our target price include: 1) a softer-than-expected China

macro economy, resulting in downside to our sales forecasts; 2) intensifying competition leading to gross margin pressures;
and 3) slower-than-expected progress of its linear guide business, causing low utilization of capacity.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

### Page 6

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

VPEC’s Jul and Aug revenue delivered double digit YoY growth, or 
23% / 25% higher than our estimate, reflecting strong AI data center 
optical networking demand, InP substrate supply getting better, and 
VPEC’s continuous customers penetration. We expect sequential 
revenues growth through Dec 2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26E revenues 
at 44% / 23% QoQ, and the product mix upgrade toward AI data 
center optical networking would continue to drive the GM 
expansion. VPEC’s July net margin up to 27% from 18% / 22% in 
3Q25 / 2Q26, reflecting the strong product mix upgrade. Maintain 
Buy with 12M TP raised to NT$793 (implied 69x 2027E PE vs. +61% 
NI YoY in 2027-28E). Read more: Global Optical Module TAM.

### Page 3

Earnings revision: We factor in VPEC’s Jul-Aug revenues and raise our 2026-28E net 
incomes by 12% / 18% / 30%, mainly on higher revenues and GMs. 2026-28E revenues 
are revised up by 7% / 14% / 25%, mainly on higher contribution from optoelectronics 
business, driven by the strong end demand of AI data center optical networking, better 
InP substrate supply, and VPEC’s customers’ expansion and product expansion from CW 
lasers to EML, along with specification upgrade. Our 2026-28E GMs are revised up on 
rising contribution from optoelectronics business, which carries a higher margin than 
other product lines. We revise up 2027-28E opex ratios, mainly on higher R&D expense 
to collaborate with key customers on next generation optical networking products.

GS vs. Bloomberg consensus: Our 2026-27E revenues are higher than Bloomberg 
consensus, reflecting our positive view on VPEC’s optoelectronics business growth. Our

towards optoelectronics. Overall, our 2026-37E net incomes are 27% / 26% higher than 
Bloomberg consensus.

at 10.5% COE (no change), and derive target PE multiple from refreshed peers’ trading 
P/E vs. forward year fundamentals (NI YoY and OPM). Based on our updated 2029-30E 
NI YoY of 18% (vs. 20% previously) and 2029-30E OPM of 38% (vs. 37% previously), and 
peers’ PEG&M of 0.4x (vs. 0.3x previously), we derive target PE multiple at 22.2x (vs. 
18.2x previously), reflecting the sector re-rating on new AI server racks ramp up driving 
optical modules specification upgrade, and InP substrate supply getting better. Our new

### Page 8

VPEC focuses on III-V compound semiconductor EPI wafer for microelectronics and 
optoelectronics devices based on InP and GaA substrate, and expands to EPI wafer for 
SiPh transceivers CW laser for data center applications. We expect strong momentum in 
VPEC growth driven by the SiPh ramp up in 2026E, microelectronics clients’ increasing 
demand, and its accumulated technology advantages on EPI wafer. We note that VPEC is 
currently trading lower than our TP implied 69x 2027E forward P/E, which is attractive, 
in our view.  
Price Target Risks and Methodology - VPEC 
Valuation: We adopt a discounted P/E method and apply a 22.2x target P/E on VPEC’s 
2029E EPS, discounting back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6% 
and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12m TP of NT$793. Our target P/E is 
based on 0.4x PEG&M ratio derived from the average level of peers in the photonics 
supply chain. 
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh business ramp up; weaker-than-expected 
microelectronics demand from smartphones clients; IDM offers EPI wafer in-house.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 35x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022. During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

E Ink Holdings Inc.  | Asia Pacific
August  Sales +20% MoM/+3%
Y oY
In this report, we focus on E Ink's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August sales were NT$3,446mn (+20% MoM/+3% YoY).
• QTD sales reached 63% of our 3Q26 revenue estimate of NT$9,985mn (-2%
QoQ/-4% YoY) and 62% of consensus at NT$10,213mn (flat QoQ/-2% YoY).
Our view:
• The solid rebound in the Aug monthly sales should alleviate the potential

risk of missing full-year guidance again (now at 10-15% YoY for 2026), in our
view, as  3Q26 revenue is tracking in line with our estimate for  a modest
decline QoQ.
• While the worst seems to be behind us and valuation is at a trough level of
12x 2027 P/E, we see no strong catalysts in the near term, despite our
positive long-term view on  e-paper demand across various applications.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
E Ink Holdings Inc. (8069.TWO)
Base case, 12x 2027e P/E. We believe this valuation methodology best captures E Ink's earn-
ings growth momentum. We view our target multiple of 12x as fair, considering our estimated
17% earnings CAGR for 2025-28. The implied PEG of 0.7x is largely in line with its typical level
in 2022-25, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 22x one-year forward consensus

EPS with a 37% earnings CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Firmer-than-expected ESL orders
n Stronger demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Better macro conditions
n Milder competition
n Brisker inventory digestion
Risks to Downside
n Softer-than-expected ESL orders
n Slower demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Macro headwinds
n Intensified competition
n More protracted inventory digestion

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below .
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

MPI Corporation  | Asia Pacific
3Q Revenue Looks Better than
Feared; OW
MPI reported August sales of NT$2,048mn, +13% M/M and +70% Y/Y, above our
NT$1,876mn estimate –  mainly supported by price hikes, TPU, and other AI ASICs.
Assuming flat September revenue M/M, 3Q revenue would rise 13% Q/Q, above our
+7% estimate and the Street at +10% Q/Q.
Stay OW: As previously indicated, we expect 3Q revenue to exceed guidance given
CPC/VPC price hikes. With very strong micro bump demand and more migration to

2nm, we believe MPI needs to expand MEMS probe card capacity to at least 8–10mn
pins per month in 2027, positioning it to meet strong AI ASIC and GPU demand.
PCB material shortages remain a risk; however, MPI is diversifying suppliers and
seeking substitutes.
For CPO,  Insertion 2 qualification continues, with insertion 3 shipments on track to
start in 4Q26.
Investors were slightly frustrated by the conservative guidance, but still view MPI as
best positioned to benefit from increased chip probing demand over the longer term

Key Takeaways
The stock could come under pressure following management's conservative
guidance. However, we expect more updates to follow in coming quarters.
Strong micro bump demand and greater migration to 2nm lead the team to argue
that MEMS probe card capacity should expand to 8–10mn pins per month in
2027.
PCB material shortages remain an explicit risk. Mitigation centers on diversifying

suppliers/seeking substitutes to reduce reliance on constrained inputs.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
                        MPI Corporation (6223.TWO)                    
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture 
the long-term value. We assume a 9.78% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium 
6.0%), a payout ratio of 67%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.0%, and a terminal growth rate 
of 4.0%. Our price target implies 50x/25x 2027e/2028e P/E. We believe its valuation will con-
tinue to climb along with growing earnings.

n Stronger-than-expected AI demand
n Stronger-than-expected market share gain from peers
n Stronger-than-expected CPO development

Risks to Downside                    
n Slower-than-expected AI demand
n Slower-than-expected market share gain from peers
n Slower-than-expected CPO development

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 120: 260911_nmr_panjit

Source PDF: all report pdf/260911_nmr_panjit.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-11

### Page 1

EQUITY: TECHNOLOGY
Panjit International   2481.TW 2481 TT
Global Markets Research
11 September 2026
Favorable pricing, product mix to elevate 2H26-2027F profit margins
New AI datacenter orders remain major growth driver
More positive on Panjit’s AI datacenter power MOSFET supply chain position 
Our recent market survey suggests sustained tight supply of select power discrete 
products in 2H26F, such as power/small signal MOSFET, and diodes. Future Electronics’ 
monthly report indicates lengthened lead times for many MOSFET and diode variants in 
Jul/Aug 2026, along with an uptrend in pricing (Fig 5). On the other hand, SDI’s (2351 TT, 
Buy) management indicated that IDM customers are placing more lead frame orders to the 
company with favorable pricing in 2H26E, in order to meet stronger AI-datacenter demand and 
to secure market share. As some products’ lead times have reached historical record levels 
(for e.g., select Schottky/switch diodes and LV/HV MOSFET lead times reached 52 weeks in 
Aug 2026, vs 20 weeks a year ago), and with limited capacity expansion among IDMs/ 
foundries in the near term, we expect the tight supply to continue in 2H26-1H27F. We also 
expect such a benign environment to be a tailwind for vendors to pass-through cost increases. 
We expect Panit’s 2H26-2027F revenue growth and margin expansion to sustain
Panjit’s 2Q26 revenue grew 16% q-q, better than management’s target, and was driven 
by improved demand across end markets. 2Q26 GM reached a 19-quarter high at 33.2% 
on a greater revenue scale, better product mix, and favorable pricing. We expect the 
revenue and profit margin q-q momentum to sustain in 2H26-2027F, as the company has 
secured more orders from the AI datacenter power discrete market, along with price hikes 
(mainly to pass-through rising fab manufacturing and material costs). We expect 3Q26-
4Q26F revenue to grow 10%/5% q-q, with AI datacenter related revenue contributing 12-14% 
total sales (vs 11% in 1H26). We also assume 2H26-2027F GM to reach 36.5%/37.3% (vs 
33.0% in 1H26) with higher AI and automotive sales exposure, better utilization rates across 
manufacturing sites, along with a third round of price hikes in 4Q26F. 
2026F/27F EPS raised by 3%/10%; TP increased to TWD214, implying 38.5% upside
We raise 2026F/27F EPS, as we assume contribution from more new AI datacenter 
orders mainly starting in 2H26-2027F, and expect a more favorable mix and pricing to 
form better margin profiles. Our TP of TWD214 is based on 25x 2027F EPS of TWD8.55 
(previous TP of TWD195 was based on 25x 2027F EPS of TWD7.79). The stock currently 
trades at 18x/16x 2027F/28F EPS.

### Page 3

Company profile
Panjit is a power semiconductor IDM headquartered in Taiwan. It currently manufactures and sells power discrete (including diodes, MOSFET, and
SIC), Power IC, and solar products. The company’s products are widely used in many end applications including industrial, green energy, consumer
electronics, automotive, computers, power supply, and communication.
Valuation Methodology
Our TP of TWD214 is based on 25x 2027F EPS of TWD8.55. We use 25x to derive our TP, vs historical P/E range of 18x since 2018 (The stock

traded in a range from 15-33x when the company was in earnings growth upcycle). The benchmark to the stock is TAIEX.
Risks that may impede the achievement of the target price
Downside risks: 1) global macroeconomic uncertainty that may drag demand for the company's products, especially auto and industrial; 2) slower-
than-expected supply chain inventory destocking; and 3) pricing competition from peers if global demand is worse than expected.
ESG

Panjit requires itself to uphold the business principles of “innovation, growth, responsibility, and sustainability” in the global semiconductor supply
chain in the face of unprecedented changes in the aspects of global environment and climate, economy and society. The company actively
promotes and continues to make improvement in environmental protection, employee safety and health, labor conditions, moral values, and the
maintenance of energy, striving to become a member of the green enterprises in the world while constantly strengthening ESG (the beauty of the

Environment, the goodness in society, and the truthfulness in governance) actions to respond to changes and challenges in the future with a high
level of dynamics.

Definition of Nomura Group's equity research rating system and sectors
The rating system is a relative system, indicating expected performance against a specific benchmark identified for each individual stock, 
subject to limited management discretion. An analyst’s target price is an assessment of the current intrinsic fair value of the stock based on an 
appropriate valuation methodology determined by the analyst. Valuation methodologies include, but are not limited to, discounted cash flow 
analysis, expected return on equity and multiple analysis. Analysts may also indicate expected absolute upside/downside relative to the stated 
target price, defined as (target price - current price)/current price.

SECTORS
A 'Bullish' stance, indicates that the analyst expects the sector to outperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Neutral' stance, 
indicates that the analyst expects the sector to perform in line with the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Bearish' stance, indicates that 
the analyst expects the sector to underperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. Sectors that are labelled as 'Not rated' or shown as 
'N/A' are not assigned ratings. Benchmarks are as follows: United States: S&P 500; Europe: Dow Jones STOXX 600; Global Emerging 
Markets (ex-Asia): MSCI Emerging Markets ex-Asia. Japan/Asia ex-Japan: Sector ratings are not assigned.

Target Price
A Target Price, if discussed, indicates the analyst’s forecast for the share price with a 12-month time horizon, reflecting in part the analyst's 
estimates for the company's earnings. The achievement of any target price may be impeded by general market and macroeconomic trends, and 
by other risks related to the company or the market, and may not occur if the company's earnings differ from estimates.

### Page 3

Earnings revision: We factor in SZS Aug revenues, revise down 2026E earnings by 11%, 
revise up 2027E earnings by 1%, and keep 2028E earnings largely unchanged. We revise 
down 2026E revenues mainly on lower-than-expected Aug revenues, considering longer 
time for new projects to reflect on company’s revenues/ earnings. We revise up 2027 
earnings mainly on higher revenues, reflecting our positive view on SZS as the key 
beneficiary of rising foldable phones.

Valuation: We are Buy rated on SZS with a 12-month target price of NT$300 (vs. 
previously at NT$286) on lower earnings. Our TP is based on a 21.9x 2027E P/E (vs. 
previously at 21.0x), which is derived from the updated sector correlation of 2027E P/E 
vs. 2027E-28E EPS YoY (Exhibit 8), with re-rating supported by positive view on rising 
foldable phone penetration. The 21.9x 2027E P/E is also within SZS’s trading range since 
Aug 2019.

### Page 4

Price Target Risks and Methodology - SZS 
Valuation: We use a near-term P/E vs. EPS growth correlation to derive our target price 
for SZS, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. Our target 2027E P/E multiple 
of 21.9x for SZS is based on the sector correlation of 2027E P/E vs. 2027-28E earnings 
growth. We thus derive our 12-month target price at NT$300. We are Buy rated. Key 
risks: Weaker-than-expected headset hinges demand; slower-than-expected foldable 
phone penetration; potential competition from more suppliers; soft market demand.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Industry View In-Line
AI-driven specification upgrades are reshaping the economics of copper-clad
laminate (CCL), the core material in PCBs, and driving demand for higher-

performance copper foil and glass cloth. We expect the global CCL TAM to rise
from US$19B in 2025 to US$47B by 2030, implying a 20% CAGR (vs market
expectations at US$35-40B), with AI/data-center applications accounting for 90%
of incremental growth and nearly two-thirds of demand by decade-end.
Content, not units, is the key growth driver. We estimate rising material intensity
accounts for 84% of AI-related CCL growth, as next-generation compute and

networking platforms move to higher-layer-count PCBs, lower-loss CCL and
smoother HVLP copper foil. Our bottom-up model implies the HVLP4 market
grows from <US$50M in 2025 to US$2.8B by 2030, a 123% CAGR. Extending the
same framework further downstream, we forecast the global PCB TAM will rise
from US$58bn to US$135bn over 2025–30, an 18% CAGR.
Broadening the AI trade upstream. We think high-end CCL, HVLP copper foil and
advanced glass cloth have entered an earnings upcycle but have yet to reach full

earnings realization and peak-cycle conditions. AI-driven specification upgrades are
reducing the number of  qualified suppliers and supporting mix, pricing and margins,
while long qualification cycles, lower yields and equipment bottlenecks should slow
the supply response.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Long qualification cycles and lower production yields in advanced copper foil and
glass cloth should keep effective high-end CCL supply tight into 2028.
Our proprietary bottom-up analysis quantifies copper foil, CCL and PCB content
across Nvidia, AMD, TPU and Trainium platforms.
We introduce a scorecard comparing 16 PCB, substrate & materials suppliers on

six factors to distinguish structural winners from cyclical beneficiaries.
DDR4-like supply squeeze; standard E-glass pricing likely has further upside as
capacity shifts toward higher-value, more advanced grade cloth.
Initiate at OW on EMC (NT$8,400 PT), TUC (NT$2,700 PT), Co-Tech (NT$730
PT), and KB Laminates (HK$75 PT).

Executive Summary
Why read this report?
We provide a comprehensive understanding of what copper foil, and copper-clad
laminates are and how they fit into the PCB supply chain. In addition, we have built
a proprietary bottom-up model to model out the copper foil and copper-clad
laminates market (please reach out to us for the model).
We also broke down the copper foil, copper-clad laminate, and printed circuit
board content per AI system across the different Nvidia, AMD, TPU, and Trainium

architectures.
AI has completely overhauled the PCB supply chain, going all the way up to upstream
materials like CCL (key raw material used in PCB production), copper foil (used in CCL
production), as well as electronic glass cloth (used in CCL production) and others. ABF
substrates are also seeing similar tailwinds, but we have talked about that here, here, and
here. In this report, we focus primarily on CCL and copper foil, but also touch on glass

cloth.
We forecast the CCL market to grow at a 20% CAGR over 2025-30, to US$47B from
US$19B (2.5x). Unit and content growth both contribute. AI data center cluster
deployments lift volumes, while faster signal transmission speeds require upgraded CCL
to contain insertion loss and crosstalk, on top of a mix shift toward higher layer-count and
more complex PCBs.
Against this demand, we see high-end CCL as under-supplied. Only a handful of suppliers

globally are qualified for M8+ grades, including EMC, Panasonic, TUC, Doosan, and
Shengyi. Because CCL capacity is grade-agnostic in architecture, nameplate capacity
overstates effective high-grade supply, and M7/M8+ supply-demand is therefore difficult
to quantify with precision. The absence of a precise gap does not change the direction. We
expect high-end tightness to translate into firmer pricing and margin expansion for the
qualified incumbents.

In copper foil, we forecast the high-end HVLP4 market to reach US$2.8B by 2030, a
123% CAGR over 2025-2030, though off a de minimis base. HVLP4 (hyper very low
profile) foil is the enabling input for low-loss, high-speed CCL, and therefore a gating item
in the AI PCB supply chain.
Effective capacity rests with five suppliers today: Mitsui Kinzoku, Co-Tech, Furukawa,
Fukuda and Copper Foil Luxembourg at roughly 1,000t/month, with Mitsui alone at 41%

of 2026 capacity. Adding to that base is harder than nameplate suggests. Converting
HVLP2 lines is not one-for-one; the longer production cycle cuts effective output by  more
than 40%. New lines require 6-12 months to ramp, and sit behind delivery queues for
surface-treatment equipment concentrated among a small number of Japanese suppliers.
We model demand rising to 39.2kt in 2027 from 12.3kt in 2026 (+218% y/y), and further
growing to 52.0kt in 2028 (+33% y/y). That would leave the market in a deficit of 31% by

### Page 5

M
Foundation
Morgan Stanley Research 5
2027 and 20% by 2028. As such, we expect a handful of qualified producers to hold
meaningful pricing power over that window.
Our conclusion is that CCL makers that are able to secure the full input set of cloth, foil
and resin should hold pricing power over downstream customers.
As a result of our bottom-up analysis of CCL and its upstream materials, we expect the
PCB TAM to grow from US$58B in 2025 to US$135B by 2030, representing an 18%

CAGR.
Beyond discovery: Why high-end CCL stocks still have room
to run
We believe the market continues to underestimate the extent to which AI
infrastructure investment will expand end demand for copper-clad laminate. Our 2030
CCL total addressable market estimate of US$47B is meaningfully above market forecasts
of US$35-40B.
The key difference lies not only in our assumptions for AI server and data-center unit
growth, but also in the increasing material intensity of next-generation computing and

networking platforms. Higher layer counts, larger and more complex PCBs, faster data-
transmission requirements, and the growing use of premium low-loss and high-speed
materials should drive CCL content per system materially higher. This is all made possible
with more advanced copper foil  with tighter surface-profile, signal-integrity, and reliability
requirements.
Structural demand is supported by capex. The spending backdrop still has more than a

year of visible growth in it. Our  house view on cash cloud capex now stands at US$1.2T in
2026 (+104% y/y) and US$1.6T  in 2027 (+38% y/y) vs. consensus at US$1.39T for 2027.
This compares with buyside expectations of US$1.8T+ (including financial and operating
leases), suggesting there could still be meaningful room for further upward consensus
revisions.
CCL content is growing faster than AI-system units. The demand driver is a specification
ladder, not a single product cycle, and that distinction is what makes the cycle durable.

Layer counts on AI server motherboards are increasing with each generation to support
additional routing space for more signals as AI boards become more complex, while rising
signal and thermal demands also support the adoption of higher grade CCLs. Crucially,
each rung multiplies rather than adds, because moving up a grade changes the resin
chemistry, the copper-foil profile and the glass-cloth grade simultaneously. Content per
rack can therefore keep rising even if unit shipment growth moderates. The continuing

migration toward lower-loss grades is another reason the earnings opportunity may have
further to run.

The real scarcity is qualified, high-yield capacity. High-speed laminate is not a
commodity that can be replaced solely on price; materials must be validated for signal

loss, thermal reliability, dimensional stability and compatibility with specific PCB
fabrication processes. For example, an upgrade to M8 grade CCL is not a simple
incremental upgrade from M7. It requires simultaneous improvements in low-loss resin,
smoother HVLP copper foil, higher-spec low-Dk glass cloth, and much tighter
manufacturing tolerances. These changes narrow the process window, reduce throughput
and yields, and increase scrap, inspection, and qualification costs. At the same time, the

pool of suppliers able to produce M8 reliably at scale is much smaller, while AI-server
demand is accelerating, creating significantly tighter effective supply. That is why the M8
CCL ASP is more than double that of M7 grade CCL (Exhibit 9).
Once a material has been designed into a high-volume platform, changing it can require
renewed signal-integrity work, reliability testing and PCB-process validation. That creates
design-in persistence, and we believe a supplier’s qualification at M9 (for example) should

be viewed as more than a one-generation design win; it creates a cumulative advantage for
the next material transition. By the time a CCL supplier is qualified for M9, it has already
demonstrated that it can meet demanding electrical, thermal, dimensional-stability, and
manufacturability requirements at commercial scale. It has also accumulated valuable
process data, refined production yields, and developed working relationships with the end
customer, PCB fabricators, and upstream material suppliers. When the end customer

begins evaluating M10 or higher-grade materials, the incumbent can build from a proven
technical and manufacturing base rather than starting the qualification process from
scratch.
This advantage is reinforced by earlier access to customer roadmaps and opportunities for
joint development. A qualified M9 supplier is more likely to understand the customer’s
next-generation loss budget, stack-up constraints, reliability requirements, and production
timeline, allowing it to tailor its M10 product before broader qualification begins.

Customers also have an incentive to work with proven suppliers because changing
materials can require renewed signal-integrity modeling, PCB-process adjustments,

### Page 7

M
Foundation
Morgan Stanley Research 7
reliability testing, and yield validation. M9 qualification therefore does not guarantee
success at M10, as each generation can reopen competition, but it meaningfully improves
the probability of qualification, shortens time to market, and creates a compounding
technological and commercial lead.

Where are we in the cycle?
In our cycle framework, we place high-end CCL and HVLP copper foil at past the trough,
but still ahead of full earnings realization and peak-cycle conditions. Demand is being

driven by a multi-year migration toward higher layer-count PCBs and lower-loss materials,
while qualified supply remains constrained by long customer qualification cycles, lower
yields and upstream bottlenecks. We therefore expect mix, pricing and margins to improve
through 2027-28; however, the opportunity remains selective, as commodity-grade CCL
can stay oversupplied even as qualified high-end materials tighten, making this a stock-
selection cycle rather than a broad sector call.

Conclusion
We view the current demand cycle as being driven by a sustained rise in material
specifications rather than by a single product refresh. This distinction is important, as it
suggests the opportunity could be more structural and durable than previous electronics
cycles, which were often more dependent on unit shipments and shorter replacement
cycles.
In our view, the specification upgrades are also multiplicative rather than merely additive.

Moving to a higher-performance grade CCL typically requires simultaneous changes across
the material stack, involving more advanced resin systems, lower-profile copper foil, and
higher-grade glass cloth. At the same time, increasingly complex AI server and networking
architectures require PCBs with more layers, greater routing density, tighter signal-
integrity tolerances, and, in some cases, larger board dimensions. As a result, the value of
CCL, copper foil, and related materials used per system, or per rack, can continue to

increase even if the pace of server or rack shipment growth begins to moderate.
The main drivers of this content growth include:
• Migration toward higher-grade CCL. Rising data-transfer speeds, higher operating
frequencies, and greater thermal loads are increasing the need for low-loss, high-
speed CCL materials. As AI accelerators and networking systems move to faster
interconnect standards, electrical performance becomes increasingly sensitive to

dielectric loss, signal attenuation, and material consistency. This should support a
continued mix shift toward higher-grade CCLs with more advanced resin
formulations and tighter manufacturing requirements.
• Increasing PCB layer counts. AI servers, accelerator boards, and high-speed
switches require substantially more routing capacity to support a growing number
of processors, memory modules, power-delivery components, and high-speed
input/output channels. Higher layer counts allow designers to accommodate this

complexity while maintaining signal and power integrity. This increases the amount
of CCL and copper foil required per board and can also raise manufacturing
complexity and average selling prices.
Taken together, the continued migration toward higher-layer-count PCBs, lower-loss and
higher-speed CCL, more advanced copper foil, and potentially larger board formats should
support sustained growth in material content per system. We believe this specification-

driven upgrade cycle creates further revenue and earnings upside for the broader CCL and
copper-foil supply chain, particularly for suppliers that are already qualified on leading AI
server, accelerator, and networking platforms. This underappreciated content opportunity
is central to our positive industry view.

Stock implications
Elite Materials Co. (2383.TW, OW, PT NT$8,400)
EMC is a global leader in high-speed, low-loss CCL, with dominant market share across all

major GPU and ASIC customer platforms. It continues to invest for future growth, with
capacity expected to grow from 5,850k sheets/month at the end of 2025 to 11,250k
sheets/month by the end of 2028, or 92% growth. Most of these capacity additions will
be to support advanced grade CCL demand (M8+) over the next few years, driven by rising
demand for AI-related systems. We therefore forecast EMC's revenue to grow at a 76%
CAGR in 2025-2028, but we expect net profit to grow at a faster rate of 117% over the

same period. On EPS, our forecasts for 2026-28 are NT$122.17, NT$266.24, and NT
$416.24, respectively.
Price target and valuation discussion: Our price target of NT$8,400 implies 20x CY28 P/
E or 0.36x PEG, which we think is fair vs. its three year average of 14x because of the
strong revenue and profit outlook driven by higher grade CCLs. In the higher grade CCL
segment, there are also less capable suppliers, so competition decreases.
The 20x CY28 P/E implied by our price target is slightly above the past 3Y average +1SD

of 19x, but much lower than the 32x recent P/E peak. We deem this valuation as fair,
because EMC is benefitting from an earnings growth cycle fueled by stronger AI server
CCL demand, EMC's own mix improvement and continued pricing adjustments, which
supports margin expansion from 29.8% in 2025 to 41.4% by 2028 and an EPS CAGR of
117% over 2025-2028, on our estimates. Our 20x CY28 P/E for EMC is also higher than our

### Page 11

M
Foundation
Morgan Stanley Research 11
17x CY28 P/E for TUC, reflecting EMC’s leading position in the AI CCL market in terms of
both technology capabilities and production capacity. The stock is trading at just 12.8x
CY28 P/E, below EMC's past 3Y average of 14x and Shengyi's current valuation of 21x
CY28 P/E. Thus, we view the risk reward as attractive.
Taiwan Union Co. (6274.TWO, OW, PT NT$2,700)
TUC is another leading supplier of high-speed, low-loss CCL in Taiwan, though behind

EMC, with particular strength in high-end networking switches at 400G, 800G and above,
as well as growing exposure to AI ASIC platforms, including AWS Trainium from Trainium2
onward. Its M7+ mix is expected to rise from approximately 36% of sales in 2025 to 46%
in 2028, while capacity grows from 2,300K sheets/month at the end of 2025 to 5,300K
sheets/month by the end of 2028, an increase of approximately 130%. The richer product
mix and pricing adjustments should support gross-margin expansion from 22.7% in 2025

to 36.0% in 2028. Our forecasts for 2026-28 EPS are NT$40.17, NT$94.43 and NT$156.68,
respectively, implying a 138% CAGR over 2025-2028.
Price target and valuation discussion: Our NT$2,700 price target implies 17x CY28 P/E,
or 0.26x PEG, vs. TUC’s three-year average of 13x. We think this premium is fair given the
rapid M7+ mix shift, capacity expansion and margin uplift, together with TUC’s strong
networking franchise and growing hyperscaler exposure. Its position as the primary, if not

sole, supplier of thick-copper CCL used by a leading data-center power-solution provider
offers an additional differentiated growth and pricing lever.
The 17x CY28 P/E implied by our price target is in line with the past 3Y average +1SD of
17x. We deem this valuation as fair, because TUC is benefitting from an earnings growth
cycle fueled by stronger AI server/networking CCL demand, TUC's own mix improvement
and continued pricing adjustments, which supports margin expansion from 22.7% in 2025

to 36.0% by 2028 and an EPS CAGR of 138% over 2025-2028, on our estimates. The stock
is trading at just 8.6x CY28 P/E, below the past 3Y average -1SD of 9.4x. Thus, we view  risk
reward as attractive.
Co-Tech (8358.TWO, OW, PT NT$730).
Co-Tech is the leading advanced  copper foil company in Taiwan and one of the few
qualified suppliers of advanced RTF and HVLP3/4 copper foil and is emerging as the most
important second source to Mitsui for AI servers and high-end networking. The company

stopped supplying conventional HTE and standard RTF in 3Q26, redirecting its capacity
toward higher-value RG and HVLP products, while its HVLP4 officially entered  the Nvidia
supply chain in 2025 and ramped up mass production in 2026. We model Co-Tech’s
HVLP4 capacity increasing from less than 50 tonnes/month in 2025 to approximately 200
tonnes at the end of 2026, 700 tonnes at the end of 2027 and 800 tonnes at the end of
2028. This would increase its global capacity share from approximately 13% in 2026 to

21% in 2028, making it the second-largest supplier after Mitsui. Our forecasts for 2026-28
EPS are NT$9.34, NT$18.59 and NT$31.63, respectively, implying a 96% CAGR over 2025-
28.
Price target and valuation discussion: Our NT$730 price target implies 23x CY28 P/E, or
0.33x PEG, vs. Co-Tech's three-year average of 20x. We think the premium is fair given Co-
Tech’s qualification moat, rapid capacity ramp and exposure to a structurally under-

### Page 12

M
Foundation
12
supplied market. We forecast global HVLP4 demand to exceed supply by approximately
31% and 20% in 2027 and 2028, respectively. Moreover, sellable HVLP4 capacity depends
on specialized surface-treatment equipment, tight process tolerances, production yields
and customer qualification, not simply installed nameplate capacity, which should limit
credible competition and support pricing power.
The 23x CY28 P/E implied by our price target is slightly above the past 3Y average of 20x.

We deem this valuation as fair, because Co-Tech is benefitting from an earnings growth
cycle fueled by stronger AI server/networking CCL demand, Co-Tech's continued mix
improvement (from HTE + RTF (RG) copper foils to advanced RTF (RG) + HVLP3+ copper
foils) and continued processing fee adjustments, which supports margin expansion from
21.8% in 2025 to 47.2% by 2028 and an EPS CAGR of 96% over 2025-2028, on our
estimates. The stock is trading at just 16.4x CY28 P/E, below Co-Tech's past 3Y average

20x. Thus, we view risk reward as attractive.
Kingboard Laminates (1888.HK, OW, PT: HK$75).
KB Laminates is the world’s largest CCL producer by volume and is differentiated by its
vertical integration across copper foil, glass yarn and cloth, epoxy resin and paper-based
materials, providing advantages in cost, supply security and production planning. Although
its current product mix remains weighted toward commodity FR-4, with less than 5% of
capacity allocated to M6+ products, KB is moving up the specification curve. Its first 350K

sheets/month of M6+ capacity is scheduled to come online in 1H27, followed by another
350K sheets/month in 1H28, while a new 21,000-tonne high-frequency and low-loss
copper-foil plant is expected to start production in mid-2027. We forecast FY25-28
revenue to grow at a 42% CAGR and net profit at a faster 91% CAGR. Our FY26-28 EPS
forecasts are HK$2.73, HK$4.51 and HK$5.40, respectively.
Price target and valuation discussion: Our HK$75 price target implies 14x CY28 P/E, or

0.7x PEG, modestly above its three-year average of 12.8x. We think this is fair given the
profit-growth outlook, the cost and supply-security benefits of vertical integration, and
the earnings optionality from its M6+ expansion. However, KB should trade at a lower
multiple than the leading high-end CCL suppliers until advanced grades become a
meaningfully larger part of its mix, as commodity FR-4 remains its core market and its
high-end qualification position is still at an earlier stage.

The 14x CY28 P/E implied by our price target is slightly above the past 3Y average of
12.8x. We deem this valuation as fair, because KB Laminates is benefitting from an
earnings growth cycle fueled by price hikes of its FR4 CCLs and glass cloth, as well as its
gradual penetration into the higher-end CCL material market, which supports margin
expansion from 19.6% in 2025 to 41.7% by 2028 and an EPS CAGR of 91% over 2025
2028, on our estimates. That said, we believe the company remains more of a cyclical play

compared to its Taiwanese peers including EMC and TUC. Thus, we view the 1x re-rating
vs. its past 3Y P/E average as fair. The stock is trading at just 8.5x CY28 P/E, below its past
3Y average of 12.8x. Therefore, we view risk reward as attractive.
To get more broad range of AI materials, please see this report: AI Infrastructure: The
Materials Super-cycle

What are the key risks? 
• A slowdown in AI infrastructure investment over the next several years. 
Demand for high-end copper foil and copper-clad laminate is increasingly tied to AI 
servers, networking equipment, and data-center expansion. A moderation in 
hyperscaler capital spending, slower growth in AI workloads, or longer server 
replacement cycles could weaken demand and delay expected capacity utilization.
• Company-specific execution or yield challenges. Lower-than-expected yields 
could result in higher unit costs and a slower margin recovery. This may create a 
gap between reported profitability and market expectations, particularly during 
the qualification and initial mass-production stages.
• Failure to qualify for next-generation materials platforms. Suppliers must 
typically undergo customer qualification processes before their products can be 
used in new server, switch, and accelerator platforms. An inability to meet 
increasingly stringent requirements for signal integrity, thermal performance, 
dimensional stability, or reliability could cause a supplier to miss an entire product 
cycle and lose share in higher-margin applications.
• Competition from Chinese suppliers. Continued investment by Chinese copper 
foil and CCL manufacturers could increase competition in higher-grade segments. 
• Constraints in upstream raw-material supply. Shortages in materials such as 
glass cloth, specialty resin, and copper foil could limit production volumes. 
• Faster-than-expected adoption of co-packaged optics. A more rapid transition to 
co-packaged optics could shorten electrical signal paths and reduce the need for 
certain high-speed PCB interconnections within networking equipment, resulting in 
a downgrade of CCL material used, therefore reducing some content opportunity 
of premium low-loss CCL and copper foil.
• Copper price changes. In estimating the TAM for copper foil and CCL, we have not 
assumed any specific movement in copper prices over the next few years. Instead, 
we have held copper prices constant at current levels.

AI-Related Demand Will Add Over US$70B to the PCB
Market
We estimate the global PCB addressable market will grow at an 18% CAGR between
2025 and 2030, increasing from US$58B to US$135B. This expansion will be driven
overwhelmingly by demand from AI and data-center infrastructure. As shown in Exhibit
22, the AI and data center-related PCB market is expected to rise from US$13B in 2025 to
US$86B by 2030, more than a sixfold increase. Its share of total PCB industry revenue

would therefore increase from roughly 20-25% in 2025 to 64% by the end of the decade.
On these assumptions, AI and data-center applications account for essentially all of the
industry’s net growth over the period, while demand across the remaining end markets is
broadly stable.
The opportunity is also expected to be diversified across end segments. Growth should
extend across PCBs used in general servers, GPU servers, custom ASIC servers,
networking switches, and optical modules as demand continues to grow across the board

and computing clusters continue to scale and grow. In addition to higher shipment
volumes, these applications carry significantly greater PCB content per system, reflecting
larger board sizes (potentially), higher layer counts, more advanced materials, tighter
electrical requirements, and more complex manufacturing processes. The combination of
volume growth and rising PCB value per system underpins the sharp expansion expected
in the AI and data-center segment through 2030.

Advanced AI PCB manufacturing requires a longer cycle
Advanced AI server PCB takes 2.2x longer to produce than a normal PCB: Based on our 
assumptions, the end-to-end lead time for an advanced AI PCB is approximately 100 days, 
or 14.6 weeks, compared with 46 days, or 6.6 weeks, for a conventional multilayer PCB. 
This represents an incremental lead time of 56 days, or eight weeks, meaning an AI PCB 
takes 2.2x as long to produce and deliver to the ODM. 
• Transportation lead times are assumed to be the same for both products, so the 
full difference reflects the longer manufacturing cycle for the advanced AI PCB. 
The additional lead time consists of approximately 15 days for glass-cloth 
production, 11 days for CCL production, and 30 days for PCB fabrication.

### Page 18

M
Foundation
18
The primary driver is the greater manufacturing complexity of the advanced AI PCB.
Higher-performance materials, including low-Dk+ glass cloth and HVLP+ copper foil,
require longer production cycles, although these materials are produced in parallel and
therefore do not add to lead time sequentially. The largest difference arises during PCB
fabrication. Our estimate assumes an Nvidia-grade compute board with 22 layers and five
lamination cycles, compared with a conventional 12–16-layer multilayer board requiring a

single lamination cycle.
Each additional HDI build-up cycle may involve another round of lay-up, lamination,
cooling, laser drilling, copper plating or via filling, imaging, and inspection before the next
layer sequence can begin. Advanced AI boards also require tighter layer-to-layer
registration, finer circuitry, stricter impedance control, and more extensive quality
assurance. Together, these requirements extend the manufacturing cycle and increase the

risk of yield loss or rework.

Regional PCB Supply Chain Scorecard
Investors often look at technology from a regional perspective, and so we
collaborated with various analysts covering different stocks to put together the below
scorecard, which we think captures the value of each company and how they position
relative to each other. We believe these six factors best capture how investors should
value these companies, in no particular order: 1) pricing power, 2) AI mix, 3) capital

efficiency, 4) market concentration, 5) supply tightness, and 6) China substitution risk.
• Pricing power. The ability to raise or maintain prices and mix through the cycle
without sacrificing volume. This is distinct from passing through input-cost
inflation, which most suppliers can do. It is the clearest evidence that a company
captures the value it creates rather than ceding it to customers. A score of 5
indicates pricing gains even in a downturn; a 1 indicates that price is the primary

basis of competition.
• AI mix. We assess the share of revenue and profit attributable to AI and
accelerated computing, including exposure to both ASIC- and GPU-based
platforms. The scoring is straightforward: a score of 5 indicates that substantially
all revenue and profit are AI-related, while a score of 1 indicates exposure of less
than 10%.
• Capital efficiency. Returns on invested capital and cash conversion measured
against the capital intensity required to grow. There are several companies that can

hold pricing power, sit in a tight market, and enjoy a genuine qualification moat,
but may differ on capital efficiency. We measure ROIC against cost of capital across
a full cycle rather than at a peak year.
• Market concentration. The degree of supplier consolidation, measured through
top-one and top-three market share, the number of suppliers customers could
realistically qualify, and the stability of those positions. Concentration matters
because it underpins many of the other factors: fragmented markets tend to

compete on price, making durable pricing power difficult to sustain.
• Supply tightness. The current and expected balance between demand and
available capacity, assessed through lead-time trends, customer prepayments and
long-term agreements. Tight supply can temporarily support pricing and margins,
making an otherwise ordinary business appear structurally stronger than it is. We
therefore place greater weight on the capacity pipeline than on current lead times:

capacity scheduled to come online over the next two years is often a better
indicator of exit-cycle margins than today’s order book.
• China substitution risk. The risk that qualified domestic Chinese suppliers or
localization policies displace revenue. This is primarily a terminal value
consideration: whether the company’s addressable market in China remains intact
over a five-year horizon. We assess China revenue exposure and its trend, the
qualification status of domestic competitors, the size and direction of the technical

gap, explicit localization targets, and barriers to entry such as tooling, intellectual
property, materials purity and export controls. The score is inverted: a 5 denotes
severe risk and is entered into the composite as 6 minus the score, reducing the
overall result.

### Page 20

M
Foundation
20
Under this framework, Ibiden scores the highest (4.50), and should be the most favored
stock in this PCB/Substrate pool. Our analyst Shoji Sato has an Overweight rating on
Ibiden, with a price target of ¥27,500. Ibiden: F3/27 1Q Results: Double Upgrade to OW (4
Aug 2026), and Shennan Circuits score the lowest (2.00). Shennan Circuits Co Ltd: 2Q26
earnings call takeaways (27 Aug 2026). We have an Equal-weight rating on Shennan
mainly due to valuation, with Shennan trading at 28x CY27 P/E vs Gold Circuit, where we

have an Overweight rating, and trades at a more favorable valuation of 15x CY27 P/E.
